# 2026-10-10 Г?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)

- **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
  historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
  explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
- **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
  decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
- **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
  `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
  into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
  observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
  and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
  vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
  `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
- **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
  `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
  `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
  `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
  and focused tests.
- **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
  backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
- **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
  for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
  runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
- **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
  the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
  project Java 21 toolchain before release.

## Validation correction Г?" 2026-10-10

The shell was switched to the installed Java 21 toolchain
(`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
`TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
`OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

## FIX-154 review-round correction Г?" 2026-10-10

- **Replay parity:** V107 now brings `trade_signal_test` forward with the
  V97 BTC freshness fields, V106 `signal_mode`, and FIX-154
  `data_quality_valid`, matching the reflection-based Replay writer.
- **Audit isolation:** audit records are snapshot and submitted to a bounded,
  one-thread executor. Replay never writes a Production audit row, and audit
  saturation/failure only increments an in-memory metric.
- **Evidence accuracy:** audit candle close times are derived from candle open
  time plus the interval, hard-confirmation is persisted, and both legacy veto
  codes are retained.
- **Startup safety:** outcome-schema verification now occurs at bean creation;
  FIX-154 verifies its required production and replay columns before runtime
  admission.
- **Validation:** Java 21 focused tests passed, including the higher-timeframe
  matrix, validation, consolidation and early outcome-schema verifier tests.

## FIX-154 closure work Г?" 2026-10-10

- Added explicit SOFT-policy tests for conservative/direct/context/consolidated
  sizing, neutral-5m non-escalation, untrusted non-bearish context, quality
  boundaries, and Replay audit suppression.
- Extended the dedicated disposable MySQL migration test to assert V107 and
  Replay-table/FIX-154 audit schema creation when the MySQL release test stage
  is run.
- Added read-only FIX-154 health counters at `/api/fix154/health`.
- The MySQL test remains an external release-stage proof: it cannot run in this
  session without the designated disposable MySQL environment variables.

## Scope correction Г?" 2026-10-10

FIX-153 reconciliation/runtime work remains postponed. The temporary changes
to its outcome-schema verifier and its MySQL integration test were reverted.
FIX-154 does not enable or alter reconciliation behavior.

## FIX-154 reviewer handoff Г?" 2026-10-10

Added `codex-docs/FIX-154_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF.md`, listing the implemented
policy, database changes, source files, tests, executed Java 21 verification,
and the remaining external MySQL/release proof.

## FIX-153 automatic reconciliation enabled Г?" 2026-10-10

- Problem: a missing historical candle left current candle ingestion healthy
  but blocked later signal generation for the affected lane.
- Root cause: the data-quality gate correctly rejects incomplete windows; the
  recovery component was present but disabled.
- Solution: enabled OFF-mode token-free reconciliation, kept its one-worker
  executor as the default, and added deterministic tests for reconnect
  evidence, bad local close-time replacement, and per-pass REST budgeting.
- Files changed: `application.yml`, `LocalCandleReconciliationConfig`, and
  `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`.
- Database impact: uses existing V108 audit/outcome tables; no data rewrite or
  backfill was executed by this change.
- Production/Replay impact: recovery is non-executable and has no Replay
  authority. Targeted Java 21 reconciliation tests passed; real-MySQL release
  tests still require the disposable test environment.

## Flyway V107 checksum recovery Г?" 2026-10-10

- Problem: startup failed because the V107 file was changed after that version
  had been applied to the local database.
- Solution: restored the exact applied V107 content and checksum
  (`-501147284`). Moved the later Replay-table columns and audit
  `hard_confirmation_present` column into forward-only V109.
- Verification: Flyway's checksum calculator matches the stored V107 value;
  `Fix154MigrationContractTest` and `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`
  pass on Java 21.

# Replay / Production Business-Logic Parity

**Revision:** RPR-0.6-DRAFT  
**Status:** NOT APPROVED Г?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
**Baseline:** `0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`; the complete dirty-worktree snapshot must be re-hashed and approved immediately before implementation.  
**Supersedes:** RPR-0.4/RPR-0.5 for review only.

## 1. Business objective and flow

Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
call candle-only output historical parity.

1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.

Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
`PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
`ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.

## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope

Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
`wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
`ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
`TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
`LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
`PaperTradingService`.

Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
approved forward-capture design is implemented.

In scope: single-symbol virtual simulation; qualifier/refusal; closed-candle
identity; virtual settings/constraints; stable Replay IDs; pure policy ports;
staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,
Production account reads, multi-symbol scheduling, and retirement of
FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
`EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.

## 3. Read/write boundary

| Class | Data |
|---|---|
| Forbidden Production account state | `wallet_asset`, `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, `wallet_settings`, account snapshots and daily account statistics. |
| Permitted read-only evidence | `candle`, `trade_signal`, `market_price_event`, `signal_processing_work`, `candle_analysis_outcome`, `order_book_snapshot`, `fix122_evaluation`, `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, `execution_opportunity`, subject to qualifier as-of rules. |
| Replay-owned writes | `regression_test_run`, `analysis_test_signal`, `wallet_execution_test`, `wallet_position_test`, and new RPR6 profile/certificate/staging objects. |

`NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` observations are deliberate
exclusions. Required accepted observations are `LEGACY`/`APPLIED`, retaining id
and ordering slot.

## 4. Complete class/file registry

| ID | Artifact | Change | Production impact |
|---|---|---|---|
| RPR6-01 | `RegressionTestWorker` | qualifier before staging; closed identity timeline | none |
| RPR6-02 | `ReplayEvidenceQualifier` new | certificate/mode/refusal | none |
| RPR6-03 | profile/store new | immutable virtual account/config | none |
| RPR6-04 | virtual-account adapter new | balances/positions/effects | none |
| RPR6-05 | `ExecutionReplayScope` | stable run keys, clocks, finally cleanup | shared; no non-Replay behavior change |
| RPR6-06 | `ShadowProductionReplayService` | staging, truthful labels, ordered calls | none |
| RPR6-07 | `TradeExecutionValidationService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
| RPR6-08 | `DynamicProfitLockService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
| RPR6-09 | `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, `PaperTradingService` | expose pure ordered inputs, no policy reorder | shared; transaction trace/regression |
| RPR6-10 | `PositionExitPolicy`, `ProfitLockPolicy`, `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` | virtual constraints, no Replay HTTP | shared; existing Production provider unchanged |
| RPR6-11 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` | experimental profile port and label | Replay only |
| RPR6-12 | `RegimeStateService` | guaranteed Replay cleanup | shared; no Production behavior change |
| RPR6-13 | `ReplayParityValidator` | analysis-only oracle | Replay only |
| RPR6-14 | next verified Flyway migration | profile/certificate/staging/trace schema | additive only |
| RPR6-15 | typed replay configuration | explicit modes and resource bounds | runtime protection |
| RPR6-16 | tests | A8 suite | none |
| RPR6-17 | docs | A10 final docs | none |

## 5. Method contract

| ID | Area | Required behavior |
|---|---|---|
| M01 | worker start | qualifier replaces the later pending-evidence write guard; it runs before any Replay business write. |
| M02 | timeline/snapshot | require `closed=1` and exact symbol/TF/open/close identity; mismatch refuses before staging. |
| M03 | context | select stored as-of evidence, historical recomputation, or refusal per dependency; never fallback to future data. |
| M04 | staging | no skip semantics. Any mid-run invariant/error discards staged artifacts and records only terminal run failure. |
| M05 | profile port | wallet/account/HTTP access in Replay throws named forbidden-dependency failure before publication. |
| M06 | order | price then signal path: advisory stop, TP without extension, label-free lock, rebase/floor, SELL/add suppression, progressive add. Simulation records price assumptions. |
| M07 | virtual budget | virtual daily-check exception ends `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`; never consults Production account data. |
| M08 | scope | clear Replay and `RegimeStateService` state in `finally` for success, cancel and error. |

## 6. Database/configuration/logging

| ID | Item | Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| D01 | migration | allocate next free migration only after read-only Flyway-history preflight; never edit V103/V106Г?"V109. |
| D02 | MySQL validation | prove baseline/upgrade; V103 collector dependency must be explicit, never silently satisfied. |
| C01 | `replay.default-mode` | `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`. |
| C02 | `replay.max-concurrent-runs` | `1`. |
| C03 | `replay.profile.required` | `true`; no wallet-settings fallback. |
| C04 | executor/pool limit | bounded; reject before harming Production freshness. |
| L01 | INFO `REPLAY_QUALIFIED` | run/mode/baseline/config/evidence counts. |
| L02 | INFO `REPLAY_REFUSED` | run/requested mode/reason codes. |
| L03 | DEBUG `REPLAY_DECISION` | run/key/route/result/evidence IDs/order/clocks. |
| L04 | WARN `REPLAY_FORBIDDEN_DEPENDENCY` | component/dependency type/run, no secret. |
| L05 | INFO `REPLAY_COMPLETED` | earned mode/rows/duration/resources. |

## 7. Trading behavior

Production policy is unchanged. ReplayГ?Ts entry/exit/add/TP/lock/stop behavior
changes only by calling the same extracted pure policies with virtual account
data. Fees/slippage are profile data and zero-fee remains explicitly labelled
when selected. Historical-only/skipped work is reproduced from durable outcome
evidence or analysis parity is refused. Same-boundary higher-timeframe
visibility requires recorded/inferred ordering; otherwise strict analysis claim
is refused.

## 8. Tests and validation

| ID | Scenario | Expected result |
|---|---|---|
| X01 | open/identity-mismatched candle | refusal before output |
| X02 | missing context/config/lineage | analysis claim refused; simulation only with declared inputs |
| X03 | excluded price event status | no false evidence failure |
| X04 | account or HTTP port | fail before publication |
| X05 | Replay signal key | stable and never equal to Production ID; near-TP/consumption works |
| X06 | Production policy fixtures | exact ordered stop/TP/lock/floor/SELL/add outcomes |
| X07 | virtual budget exception | named failed run, no account read |
| X08 | experimental observer | baseline never invokes; output labelled experimental |
| X09 | cancellation/error | state cleanup and staged artifact discard |
| X10 | concurrent Replay/load | one-run/pool bounds preserve freshness |
| X11 | real MySQL migration | next migration, B105/V103 policy and upgrade path proven |
| X12 | baseline hash | CI rejects mismatched source/config request |

## 9. Risks and rollback

Risks: policy drift through shared extraction, latency, look-ahead, Flyway
collision and false labels. Mitigate with pure ports, Production regression and
transaction tests, qualification refusal, preflight and mode metrics. Rollback
disables the new Replay route; it never falls back to wallet reads. Schema is
additive and corrected only forward.

## 10. Documentation and reconciliation

After approved implementation and validation only, update relevant
`codex-docs` and `claude-docs` architecture/changelog/trading/investigation
documents. Shared knowledge/decisions change only through dual agreement.
Implementation report must map every RPR6/M/D/C/L/X item and every actual file
to this registry; any missing or unregistered material work rejects acceptance.

Unresolved disagreements: NOT REVIEWED

the required PDF artifact, and explicitly approved by the user.

--- agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md ---
1	# Replay / Production Business-Logic Parity
2	
3	**Revision:** RPR-0.6-DRAFT  
4	**Status:** NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
5	**Baseline:** `0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`; the complete dirty-worktree snapshot must be re-hashed and approved immediately before implementation.  
6	**Supersedes:** RPR-0.4/RPR-0.5 for review only.
7	
8	## 1. Business objective and flow
9	
10	Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
11	virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
12	call candle-only output historical parity.
13	
14	1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
15	2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
16	3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
17	4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
18	5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
19	6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.
20	
21	Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
22	`PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
23	`ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
24	they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.
25	
26	## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope
27	
28	Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
29	`wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
30	Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
31	`ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
32	`TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
33	`LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
34	`PaperTradingService`.
35	
36	Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
37	order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
38	windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
39	approved forward-capture design is implemented.
40	
41	In scope: single-symbol virtual simulation; qualifier/refusal; closed-candle
42	identity; virtual settings/constraints; stable Replay IDs; pure policy ports;
43	staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,
44	Production account reads, multi-symbol scheduling, and retirement of
45	FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
46	`EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.
47	
48	## 3. Read/write boundary
49	
50	| Class | Data |
51	|---|---|
52	| Forbidden Production account state | `wallet_asset`, `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, `wallet_settings`, account snapshots and daily account statistics. |
53	| Permitted read-only evidence | `candle`, `trade_signal`, `market_price_event`, `signal_processing_work`, `candle_analysis_outcome`, `order_book_snapshot`, `fix122_evaluation`, `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, `execution_opportunity`, subject to qualifier as-of rules. |
54	| Replay-owned writes | `regression_test_run`, `analysis_test_signal`, `wallet_execution_test`, `wallet_position_test`, and new RPR6 profile/certificate/staging objects. |
55	
56	`NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` observations are deliberate
57	exclusions. Required accepted observations are `LEGACY`/`APPLIED`, retaining id
58	and ordering slot.
59	
60	## 4. Complete class/file registry
61	
62	| ID | Artifact | Change | Production impact |
63	|---|---|---|---|
64	| RPR6-01 | `RegressionTestWorker` | qualifier before staging; closed identity timeline | none |
65	| RPR6-02 | `ReplayEvidenceQualifier` new | certificate/mode/refusal | none |
66	| RPR6-03 | profile/store new | immutable virtual account/config | none |
67	| RPR6-04 | virtual-account adapter new | balances/positions/effects | none |
68	| RPR6-05 | `ExecutionReplayScope` | stable run keys, clocks, finally cleanup | shared; no non-Replay behavior change |
69	| RPR6-06 | `ShadowProductionReplayService` | staging, truthful labels, ordered calls | none |
70	| RPR6-07 | `TradeExecutionValidationService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
71	| RPR6-08 | `DynamicProfitLockService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
72	| RPR6-09 | `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, `PaperTradingService` | expose pure ordered inputs, no policy reorder | shared; transaction trace/regression |
73	| RPR6-10 | `PositionExitPolicy`, `ProfitLockPolicy`, `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` | virtual constraints, no Replay HTTP | shared; existing Production provider unchanged |
74	| RPR6-11 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` | experimental profile port and label | Replay only |
75	| RPR6-12 | `RegimeStateService` | guaranteed Replay cleanup | shared; no Production behavior change |
76	| RPR6-13 | `ReplayParityValidator` | analysis-only oracle | Replay only |
77	| RPR6-14 | next verified Flyway migration | profile/certificate/staging/trace schema | additive only |
78	| RPR6-15 | typed replay configuration | explicit modes and resource bounds | runtime protection |
79	| RPR6-16 | tests | A8 suite | none |
80	| RPR6-17 | docs | A10 final docs | none |
81	
82	## 5. Method contract
83	
84	| ID | Area | Required behavior |
85	|---|---|---|
86	| M01 | worker start | qualifier replaces the later pending-evidence write guard; it runs before any Replay business write. |
87	| M02 | timeline/snapshot | require `closed=1` and exact symbol/TF/open/close identity; mismatch refuses before staging. |
88	| M03 | context | select stored as-of evidence, historical recomputation, or refusal per dependency; never fallback to future data. |
89	| M04 | staging | no skip semantics. Any mid-run invariant/error discards staged artifacts and records only terminal run failure. |
90	| M05 | profile port | wallet/account/HTTP access in Replay throws named forbidden-dependency failure before publication. |
91	| M06 | order | price then signal path: advisory stop, TP without extension, label-free lock, rebase/floor, SELL/add suppression, progressive add. Simulation records price assumptions. |
92	| M07 | virtual budget | virtual daily-check exception ends `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`; never consults Production account data. |
93	| M08 | scope | clear Replay and `RegimeStateService` state in `finally` for success, cancel and error. |
94	
95	## 6. Database/configuration/logging
96	
97	| ID | Item | Requirement |
98	|---|---|---|
99	| D01 | migration | allocate next free migration only after read-only Flyway-history preflight; never edit V103/V106ƒ?"V109. |
100	| D02 | MySQL validation | prove baseline/upgrade; V103 collector dependency must be explicit, never silently satisfied. |
101	| C01 | `replay.default-mode` | `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`. |
102	| C02 | `replay.max-concurrent-runs` | `1`. |
103	| C03 | `replay.profile.required` | `true`; no wallet-settings fallback. |
104	| C04 | executor/pool limit | bounded; reject before harming Production freshness. |
105	| L01 | INFO `REPLAY_QUALIFIED` | run/mode/baseline/config/evidence counts. |
106	| L02 | INFO `REPLAY_REFUSED` | run/requested mode/reason codes. |
107	| L03 | DEBUG `REPLAY_DECISION` | run/key/route/result/evidence IDs/order/clocks. |
108	| L04 | WARN `REPLAY_FORBIDDEN_DEPENDENCY` | component/dependency type/run, no secret. |
109	| L05 | INFO `REPLAY_COMPLETED` | earned mode/rows/duration/resources. |
110	
111	## 7. Trading behavior
112	
113	Production policy is unchanged. Replayƒ?Ts entry/exit/add/TP/lock/stop behavior
114	changes only by calling the same extracted pure policies with virtual account
115	data. Fees/slippage are profile data and zero-fee remains explicitly labelled
116	when selected. Historical-only/skipped work is reproduced from durable outcome
117	evidence or analysis parity is refused. Same-boundary higher-timeframe
118	visibility requires recorded/inferred ordering; otherwise strict analysis claim
119	is refused.
120	
121	## 8. Tests and validation
122	
123	| ID | Scenario | Expected result |
124	|---|---|---|
125	| X01 | open/identity-mismatched candle | refusal before output |
126	| X02 | missing context/config/lineage | analysis claim refused; simulation only with declared inputs |
127	| X03 | excluded price event status | no false evidence failure |
128	| X04 | account or HTTP port | fail before publication |
129	| X05 | Replay signal key | stable and never equal to Production ID; near-TP/consumption works |
130	| X06 | Production policy fixtures | exact ordered stop/TP/lock/floor/SELL/add outcomes |
131	| X07 | virtual budget exception | named failed run, no account read |
132	| X08 | experimental observer | baseline never invokes; output labelled experimental |
133	| X09 | cancellation/error | state cleanup and staged artifact discard |
134	| X10 | concurrent Replay/load | one-run/pool bounds preserve freshness |
135	| X11 | real MySQL migration | next migration, B105/V103 policy and upgrade path proven |
136	| X12 | baseline hash | CI rejects mismatched source/config request |
137	
138	## 9. Risks and rollback
139	
140	Risks: policy drift through shared extraction, latency, look-ahead, Flyway
141	collision and false labels. Mitigate with pure ports, Production regression and
142	transaction tests, qualification refusal, preflight and mode metrics. Rollback
143	disables the new Replay route; it never falls back to wallet reads. Schema is
144	additive and corrected only forward.
145	
146	## 10. Documentation and reconciliation
147	
148	After approved implementation and validation only, update relevant
149	`codex-docs` and `claude-docs` architecture/changelog/trading/investigation
150	documents. Shared knowledge/decisions change only through dual agreement.
151	Implementation report must map every RPR6/M/D/C/L/X item and every actual file
152	to this registry; any missing or unregistered material work rejects acceptance.
153	
154	## 11. Approval

491	        Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
492	        // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
493	        // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
494	        // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
495	        // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().

502	                        + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
503	            }
504	        }
505	        log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
506	        return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);

509	    private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
510	        return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
511	                symbol, interval, start, end);
512	    }

514	    private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
515	                                      int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
516	        int resolved = 0;
517	        int size = candles.size();
518	        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
519	            checkStopRequested(runId);
520	            Candle candle = candles.get(i);
521	            List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
522	                    symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
523	            if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
524	                resolved++;
525	            }
526	            if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
527	                int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
528	                updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
529	                        "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
530	            }
531	        }
532	        return resolved;
533	    }
534	
535	    private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
536	            long runId,
537	            String symbol,
538	            Instant executionStart,
539	            Instant executionEnd,
540	            List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
541	            List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
542	            List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
543	            String btcSymbol,
544	            List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
545	            List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
546	            List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
547	            ReplayDataSource dataSource,
548	            ReplayDataset replayDataset
549	    ) {
550	        List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
551	        oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
552	        fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
553	        oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
554	        btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
555	        btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
556	        btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
557	        timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
558	                .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
559	                .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
560	                .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
561	
562	        int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
563	        int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
564	        int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
565	        java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
566	
567	        // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
568	        // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
569	        // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
570	        ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
571	        // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
572	        // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
573	        analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
574	        try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
575	        // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
576	        // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
577	        // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
578	        // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
579	        // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
580	        for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
581	            checkStopRequested(runId);
582	            ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);

609	                // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.

626	                    else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
627	                    else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
628	
629	                    if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
630	                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
631	                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
632	                    else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;

702	                try {

704	                            "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);

193	                    cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
194	                    realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);

196	                    if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;

200	                    // opportunity evidence boundary as production before any new BUY can form.
201	                    replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
202	                    executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());

218	                    if (priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, open.entryTime())) {

220	                                runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m);
221	                        open = nearTpFallback.position();

223	                            cash = cash.add(nearTpFallback.partialProceeds(), MC);
224	                            realized = realized.add(nearTpFallback.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
225	                        }

227	                    persistManagement(runId, signal, open.profitLockActive() ? "PROFIT_LOCK_ACTIVE" : "POSITION_HOLD",
228	                            open.takeProfit(), open.takeProfit(), open, exit.explanation());
229	                }
230	            }
231

233	                // Production's candle-close PaperTradingService has one additional SELL authority

242	                    BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
243	                    cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
244	                    realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);

562	    private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
563	        BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
564	        if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
565	
566	        // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
567	        // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
568	        // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
569	        boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
570	        if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
571	            // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
572	            // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
573	            PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
574	                    oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
575	                    p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
576	                    p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
577	            if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
578	                BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
579	                BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
580	                persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
581	                jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
582	                return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
583	            }
584	            oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
585	                    runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
586	                    p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
587	                    p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
588	                    p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
589	                    continuation.explanation());
590	            persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
591	            return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
592	        }
593	        // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
594	        // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
595	        // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
596	        if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
597	            return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
598	
599	        ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
600	        if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
601	                || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
602	                || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
603	                || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
604	                || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
605	            BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
606	                    ? BigDecimal.ZERO
607	                    : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
608	                            .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
609	                            .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
610	            log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
611	                            "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
612	                    runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
613	                    updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
614	                    p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
615	                    updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
616	                            && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
617	        }
618	        BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
619	        if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
620	                && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
621	            String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
622	                    + profitLockConfigText();
623	            log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
624	                    runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
625	            persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
626	            return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
627	        }
628	        if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
629	                && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
630	            log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
631	                    runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
632	                    updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
633	            if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
634	                String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
635	                        + profitLockConfigText();
636	                persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
637	                return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
638	            }
639	            PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
640	                    oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
641	            if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
642	                String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
643	                persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
644	                return ExitDecision.hold();
645	            }
646	            String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
647	                    + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
648	            persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
649	            return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
650	        }
651	        // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
652	        // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
653	        // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
654	        PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
655	                oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
656	        if (normalExit.exit()) {
657	            return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
658	        }
659	        if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
660	            TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
661	            if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
662	                return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
663	            }
664	        }
665	        return ExitDecision.hold();
666	    }
667	
668	    private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
669	                                                                    TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
670	                                                                    TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
671	        NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
672	                current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
673	                current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
674	        NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
675	                state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
810	        if (favorable.signum() < 0) favorable = BigDecimal.ZERO;
811	        return favorable.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
812	                .divide(target.subtract(entry, MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
813	    }

842	        if (allocationPercent <= 0) return "NONE";
843	        if (allocationPercent < 50) return "SCOUT_ENTRY";
844	        if (allocationPercent < 100) return "CONFIRMATION_ADD";

848	    private ShadowPosition addToPosition(long runId, ShadowPosition open, TradeSignal signal,
849	                                         BigDecimal addedQty, BigDecimal addedCost, int addPercent) {
850	        BigDecimal newQuantity = open.quantity().add(addedQty, MC);
851	        BigDecimal newCost = open.cost().add(addedCost, MC);
852	        BigDecimal newEntry = newCost.divide(newQuantity, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
853	        BigDecimal newStop = open.stopLoss();
854	        if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && (newStop == null || signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(newStop) > 0)) {
855	            newStop = signal.getStopLoss();
856	        }
857	        BigDecimal newTakeProfit = open.takeProfit();
858	        if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && (newTakeProfit == null || signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(newTakeProfit) > 0)) {
859	            newTakeProfit = signal.getTakeProfit();
860	        }
861	        int newPercent = Math.min(100, open.positionPercent() + addPercent);
862	        jdbcTemplate.update("""
863	                UPDATE wallet_position_test
864	                SET entry_price=?, quantity=?, total_cost_usdt=?, position_percent=?,
865	                    stop_loss_usdt=?, take_profit_usdt=?
866	                WHERE id=? AND test_run_id=?
867	                """, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
868	                open.positionId(), runId);
869	        ShadowPosition updated = open.withAdd(newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit);
870	        persistNearTpState(updated);
871	        return updated;
872	    }

1	package com.crypto.execution.service;
2	
3	import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
4	import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
5	import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
6	
7	import java.time.Instant;
8	import java.util.ArrayList;

11	import java.util.Optional;

13	import java.util.Set;
14	import java.util.function.Consumer;

16	/**
17	 * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
18	 *
19	 * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
20	 * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
21	 * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
22	 * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
23	 */
24	@Component
25	public class ExecutionReplayScope {
26	
27	    /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
28	     * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
29	    public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
30	
31	    private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
32	
33	    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
34	        return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
35	    }
36	
37	    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
38	                      ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
39	        if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
40	        List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
41	                .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
42	                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
43	                .toList();
44	        state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
45	        return new Scope();
46	    }
47	
48	    /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
49	    public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
50	        if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
51	        State s=required();
52	        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
53	        if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
54	            s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
55	    }
56	    public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
57	        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
58	        return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
59	    }
60	    public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
61	    public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
62	    public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
63	
64	    public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
65	    public long runId() { return required().runId; }
66	    public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
67	    public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
68	    public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
69	
70	    public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
71	
72	    /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
73	    public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
74	        State s = required();
75	        s.reference = observedAt;
76	        s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
77	    }
78	
79	    public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
80	        State s = required();
81	        return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
82	    }
83	
84	    /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
85	    public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
86	        return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
87	    }
88	
89	    /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
90	    public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
91	        if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
92	    }
93	
94	    /**
95	     * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
96	     * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
97	     * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
98	     */
99	    public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
100	        if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
101	        State s = required();
102	        s.signals.add(signal);
103	        s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
104	    }

107	        State s = required();
108	        Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
109	        if (ref == null) return List.of();
110	        return s.signals.stream()

1	package com.crypto.service;

22	/**
23	 * Execution-only multi-timeframe validation.
24	 *
25	 * Frozen responsibilities:
26	 *  - 1m: the only normal execution timeframe
27	 *  - 5m: tactical confirmation
28	 *  - 1h: strategic filter/veto
29	 *
30	 * Execution profiles affect only entry eligibility and position percentage.
31	 * They never modify indicators, scores, or FinalDecisionService output.
32	 */
33	@Slf4j
34	@Service
35	@RequiredArgsConstructor
36	public class TradeExecutionValidationService {
37	
38	    private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
39	    private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
40	    private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
41	    private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
42	    private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
43	    private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
44	
45	    // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
46	    // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
47	    // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
48	    // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
49	    private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;
50	
51	    private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
52	    private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
53	    private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
54	    private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
55	    @Autowired(required = false)
56	    private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
57	    @Autowired(required = false)
58	    private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
59	    @Autowired(required = false)
60	    private Fix154Metrics fix154Metrics;
61	
62	    public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
63	        ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
64	        if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
65	
66	        WalletSettings settings = settings();
67	        if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
68	            TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
69	            // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
70	            // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
71	            // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
72	            if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
73	                    && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
74	                    && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
75	                return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
76	                        "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
77	                                + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
78	            }
79	        }
80	
81	        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
82	        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
83	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
84	                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
85	        }
86	
87	        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
88	        if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
89	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
90	                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
91	        }
92	
93	        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
94	        if(!assessment.allowed()) {
95	            recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
96	            audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
97	            return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
98	        }
99	
100	        ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
101	        ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
102	            case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
103	            // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
104	            // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
105	            // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
106	            case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
107	                    executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
108	            case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
109	        };
110	        ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
111	        audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
112	        return result;
113	    }
114	
115	    /**
116	     * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
117	     * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
118	     * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
119	     * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
120	     * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
121	     * normal BUY path would accept.
122	     */
123	    public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
124	        return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
125	    }
126	
127	    /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
128	    public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
129	        if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
130	            return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
131	        }
132	
133	        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
134	        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
135	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
136	                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
137	        }
138	
139	        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
140	        if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
141	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
142	                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
143	        }
144	
145	        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
146	        if(!assessment.allowed()) {
147	            recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
148	            audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
149	            return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
150	        }
151	
152	        ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
153	        TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
154	        TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
155	        ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
156	            case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
157	            case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
158	            case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
159	        };
160	        ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
161	        audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
162	        return result;
163	    }
164	
165	    public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
166	        ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
167	        if (!base.allowed()) return base;
168	
169	        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
170	        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
171	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
172	                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m SELL signal.");
173	        }
174	        if (!isBearish(fiveMinute.getDecision())) {
175	            return ValidationResult.reject("5M_NOT_BEARISH",

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/DynamicProfitLockService.java ---
315	                nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
316	                nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
317	        );
318	    }

321	            String name,

327	    private WalletSettings settings() {
328	        return settingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> settingsRepository.save(
329	                WalletSettings.builder()
330	                        .id(1L)

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java ---
1	package com.crypto.wallet.service;
2	
3	import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
4	import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
5	import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
6	import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
7	
8	import java.math.BigDecimal;
9	import java.math.MathContext;
10	import java.util.Locale;
11	
12	/**
13	 * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
14	 *
15	 * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
16	 * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
17	 * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
18	 * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
19	 *
20	 * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
21	 * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
22	 */
23	@Component
24	@RequiredArgsConstructor
25	@Slf4j
26	public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
27	    private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
28	
29	    private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
30	    public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
31	        if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
32	                || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
33	            return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
34	        }
35	        String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
36	        BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
37	        BigDecimal minimum;
38	        try {
39	            minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
40	        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
41	            // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
42	            // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
43	            log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
44	                    pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
45	            return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
46	        }
47	        if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
48	            return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
49	        }
50	        return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
51	    }
52	
53	    public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
54	    }
55	}

143	    /**
144	     * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
145	     * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
146	     */
147	    @Transactional
148	    public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
149	        IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
150	        TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
151	        // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
152	        signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
153	        return signalRepository.save(signal);
154	    }
155	
156	    /**
157	     * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
158	     * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
159	     * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
160	     *
161	     * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
162	     * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
163	     * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
164	     * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
165	     */
166	    @Transactional
167	    public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
168	        if (indicator == null) {
169	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
170	        }
171	        IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
172	        Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
173	        TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
174	        signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
175	        return signalRepository.save(signal);
176	    }

1	package com.crypto.regression.service;
2	
3	import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
4	import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
5	
6	import java.util.ArrayList;
7	import java.util.List;
8	import java.util.Objects;
9	
10	/**
11	 * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
12	 * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
13	 */
14	@Service
15	public class ReplayParityValidator {
16	
17	    public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
18	        List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
19	        if (production == null || replay == null) {
20	            mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
21	            return new Result(false, mismatches);
22	        }
23	
24	        same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
25	        same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
26	        same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
27	        same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
28	        same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
29	        same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
30	        same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
31	        same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
32	        same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
33	        same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
34	        same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
35	        same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
36	        same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
37	                production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
38	        return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
39	    }
40	
41	    private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
42	        if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
43	            mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
44	        }
45	    }
46	
47	    public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
48	}

1	package com.crypto.execution.service;
2	
3	import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
4	import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
5	import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
6	
7	import java.time.Instant;
8	import java.util.ArrayList;
9	import java.util.Comparator;
10	import java.util.List;
11	import java.util.Optional;
12	import java.util.HashSet;
13	import java.util.Set;
14	import java.util.function.Consumer;
15	
16	/**
17	 * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
18	 *
19	 * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
20	 * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
21	 * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
22	 * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
23	 */
24	@Component
25	public class ExecutionReplayScope {
26	
27	    /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
28	     * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
29	    public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
30	
31	    private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
32	
33	    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
34	        return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
35	    }
36	
37	    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
38	                      ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
39	        if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
40	        List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
41	                .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
42	                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
43	                .toList();
44	        state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
45	        return new Scope();
46	    }
47	
48	    /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
49	    public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
50	        if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
51	        State s=required();
52	        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
53	        if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
54	            s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
55	    }
56	    public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
57	        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
58	        return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
59	    }
60	    public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
61	    public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
62	    public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
63	
64	    public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
65	    public long runId() { return required().runId; }
66	    public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
67	    public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
68	    public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
69	
70	    public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
71	
72	    /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
73	    public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
74	        State s = required();
75	        s.reference = observedAt;

841	    private String replayStage(int allocationPercent) {
842	        if (allocationPercent <= 0) return "NONE";
843	        if (allocationPercent < 50) return "SCOUT_ENTRY";
844	        if (allocationPercent < 100) return "CONFIRMATION_ADD";
845	        return "TREND_ADD";
846	    }

848	    private ShadowPosition addToPosition(long runId, ShadowPosition open, TradeSignal signal,
849	                                         BigDecimal addedQty, BigDecimal addedCost, int addPercent) {
850	        BigDecimal newQuantity = open.quantity().add(addedQty, MC);
851	        BigDecimal newCost = open.cost().add(addedCost, MC);
852	        BigDecimal newEntry = newCost.divide(newQuantity, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
853	        BigDecimal newStop = open.stopLoss();
854	        if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && (newStop == null || signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(newStop) > 0)) {
855	            newStop = signal.getStopLoss();
856	        }
857	        BigDecimal newTakeProfit = open.takeProfit();
858	        if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && (newTakeProfit == null || signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(newTakeProfit) > 0)) {
859	            newTakeProfit = signal.getTakeProfit();
860	        }
861	        int newPercent = Math.min(100, open.positionPercent() + addPercent);
862	        jdbcTemplate.update("""
863	                UPDATE wallet_position_test
864	                SET entry_price=?, quantity=?, total_cost_usdt=?, position_percent=?,
865	                    stop_loss_usdt=?, take_profit_usdt=?
866	                WHERE id=? AND test_run_id=?
867	                """, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
868	                open.positionId(), runId);
869	        ShadowPosition updated = open.withAdd(newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit);
870	        persistNearTpState(updated);
871	        return updated;
872	    }

151	    public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
152	        Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
153	            Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
154	        if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
155	        // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
156	        if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
157	        Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
158	        if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
159	            Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
160	                Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
161	            if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
162	        }

195	        WalletSettings s = walletSettingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
196	                .id(1L).dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true).profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))

201	                nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
202	                nvl(s.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
203	        return v.withLock(st.highestPrice(), st.active(), st.lockPrice());

17	@Service
18	@RequiredArgsConstructor
19	public class CandleDataQualityService {
20	
21	    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
22	    private final AnalysisScoringProperties properties;

24	    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
25	    public CandleDataQualityResult validate(String symbol, String intervalCode) {
26	        int minimum = properties.dataQuality().minimumCandles();
27	        List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
28	                symbol, intervalCode, PageRequest.of(0, minimum)
29	        ));
30	        candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));
31	
32	        List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
33	        int invalid = 0;
34	        for (Candle candle : candles) {
35	            boolean invalidOhlc = candle.getOpenPrice() == null
36	                    || candle.getHighPrice() == null
37	                    || candle.getLowPrice() == null
38	                    || candle.getClosePrice() == null
39	                    || candle.getVolume() == null
40	                    || candle.getOpenPrice().signum() <= 0
41	                    || candle.getHighPrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
42	                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
43	                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getHighPrice()) > 0
44	                    || candle.getVolume().signum() < 0;
45	            if (invalidOhlc) {
46	                invalid++;
47	            }
48	        }
49	
50	        Duration expectedStep = intervalDuration(intervalCode);
51	        int missing = 0;
52	        if (!expectedStep.isZero()) {
53	            for (int index = 1; index < candles.size(); index++) {
54	                Duration actual = Duration.between(candles.get(index - 1).getOpenTime(), candles.get(index).getOpenTime());
55	                if (actual.compareTo(expectedStep) > 0) {
56	                    long steps = actual.toMillis() / expectedStep.toMillis();
57	                    missing += Math.max(0, (int) steps - 1);
58	                }
59	            }
60	        } else {
61	            warnings.add("Unsupported interval for gap validation: " + intervalCode);
62	        }
63	
64	        if (candles.size() < minimum) {
65	            warnings.add("Insufficient history: " + candles.size() + "/" + minimum + " closed candles");
66	        }
67	        if (missing > 0) {
68	            warnings.add("Missing " + missing + " candle(s) in the analysis window");
69	        }
70	        if (invalid > 0) {
71	            warnings.add("Found " + invalid + " candle(s) with invalid OHLCV data");
72	        }
73	
74	        boolean valid = candles.size() >= minimum
75	                && missing <= properties.dataQuality().maximumMissingCandles()
76	                && (!properties.dataQuality().blockOnInvalidOhlc() || invalid == 0);
77	
78	        return new CandleDataQualityResult(valid, minimum, candles.size(), missing, invalid, List.copyOf(warnings));
79	    }
