601	                        saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
602	                                "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
603	                    }
604	                    continue;
605	                }
606	
607	                // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
608	                // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
609	                // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
610	                if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
611	
612	                replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
613	                operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
614	                TradeSignal generated;
615	                try {
616	                    generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
617	                } finally {
618	                    profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
619	                }
620	                replayScope.appendSignal(generated);

736	    /**
737	     * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
738	     * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
739	     * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
740	     */
741	    private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
742	        try {
743	            java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
744	            java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
745	            columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
746	            // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
747	            // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
748	            // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
749	            columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
750	            columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
751	            columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
752	
753	            for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
754	                if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
755	                Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
756	                if (column == null) continue;
757	                field.setAccessible(true);
758	                Object value = field.get(signal);
759	                if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
760	                if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
761	                String columnName = column.name();
762	                if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
763	                columns.add(columnName);
764	                values.add(value);
765	            }
766	
767	            String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
768	            jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
769	        } catch (Exception e) {
770	            throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
771	        }
772	    }
773	
774	    private String snakeCase(String value) {
775	        return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
776	    }
777	
778	    private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
779	        return switch (interval) {
780	            case "1m" -> 60L;
781	            case "5m" -> 300L;
782	            case "1h" -> 3600L;
783	            default -> 60L;
784	        };
785	    }

789	            case "1h" -> 0;
790	            case "5m" -> 1;

51	    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired private com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService priceEvents;
52	    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
53	    @Transactional(isolation=org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
54	    public void onPrice(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observedAt,long eventId){
55	        price=com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(price);
56	        if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
57	        // Acquire the existing managed-position lock BEFORE freshness, including waits on it.
58	        try {managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol,"OPEN");}
59	        catch(RuntimeException failure){
60	            // FIX-153: preserve only the established pre-business position-deadlock retry permission.
61	            if(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure))
62	                throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
63	            throw failure;
64	        }
65	        Instant localNow=Instant.now();
66	        String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
67	        if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
68	        if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
69	        onPrice(symbol,price);
70	        // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
71	        priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
72	    }

89	                throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);

142	            if (authoritativePrice && (profitLock.active() || profitLock.triggered() || profitLock.rebasing())) {

233	                        }

235	                        // FIX-056: progressive adds use the same fresh canonical price authority
236	                        // as initial entries. Signal.latestPrice remains decision-time evidence only.
237	                        ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
238	                                .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
239	                        if (fresh == null) {
240	                            log.info("Progressive BUY skipped: no fresh execution price for {}", symbol);

242	                        }
243	                        ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision freshDecision =
244	                                executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, addDecision, fresh.price());
245	                        if (!freshDecision.allowed()) return Optional.of(position);
246	                        addPercent = freshDecision.positionPercent();
247	                        ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
248	                                executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, fresh.price());
249	                        boolean walletAdded = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(
250	                                signal, fresh.price(), fresh.observedAt(), addPercent,
251	                                "Execution Intelligence [" + freshDecision.source() + "] " + freshDecision.explanation()
252	                                        + " FreshPrice=" + fresh.price() + "@" + fresh.observedAt(),
253	                                freshDecision.source(),
254	                                entryQuality.score());
255	                        if (walletAdded) {
256	                            BigDecimal addedQuantity = walletTradeRepository

258	                                            signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")

260	                                    .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
261	                                    .orElse(BigDecimal.ZERO);
262	                            if (addedQuantity.signum() > 0) {
263	                                BigDecimal oldCost = position.getEntryPrice().multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
264	                                BigDecimal addCost = fresh.price().multiply(addedQuantity, MC);
265	                                BigDecimal newQuantity = position.getQuantity().add(addedQuantity);

310	            executionDecision = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(signal);
311	        }
312	
313	        if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
314	            log.info("Execution Intelligence did not open a BUY: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, state={}, source={}, code={}, evidenceScore={}, buys={}, watches={}, detail={}",
315	                    signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), executionDecision.state(),
316	                    executionDecision.source(), executionDecision.code(),
317	                    executionDecision.evidence().evidenceScore(), executionDecision.evidence().buyCount(),
318	                    executionDecision.evidence().watchCount(), executionDecision.explanation());
319	            return Optional.empty();
320	        }
321	
322	        if (positionRepository.countByStatus(PositionStatus.OPEN) >= properties.maxOpenPositions()) {
323	            return Optional.empty();
324	        }
325	
326	        enforceDailyLossLimit();
327	
328	        int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0
329	                ? 100
330	                : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
331	        int executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
332	        int effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
333	                Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
334	        BigDecimal positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
335	                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
336	        BigDecimal riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
337	                .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
338	                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
339	                .multiply(positionScale, MC);
340	        // FIX-056: resolve a canonical execution-time price AFTER all signal/HTF gates but
341	        // BEFORE risk sizing and wallet persistence. Never size from a stale signal snapshot.
342	        ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice freshExecutionPrice = executionPriceAuthorityService
343	                .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
344	        if (freshExecutionPrice == null) {
345	            log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but no fresh canonical execution price was available; BUY skipped.",
346	                    executionSignal.getId());
347	            return Optional.empty();
348	        }
349	        executionDecision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(
350	                executionSignal, executionDecision, freshExecutionPrice.price());
351	        if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
352	            log.info("BUY revalidation rejected signal {} at fresh price {}: {} - {}",
353	                    executionSignal.getId(), freshExecutionPrice.price(), executionDecision.code(), executionDecision.explanation());
354	            return Optional.empty();
355	        }
356	        // Recompute exposure after fresh-price Entry Quality because the final guard may
357	        // reduce the approved allocation further than the signal-time decision did.
358	        executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
359	        effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
360	                Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
361	        positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
362	                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
363	        riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
364	                .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
365	                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
366	                .multiply(positionScale, MC);
367	        BigDecimal riskPerUnit = freshExecutionPrice.price().subtract(executionSignal.getStopLoss(), MC).abs();
368	
369	        if (riskPerUnit.signum() == 0) {
370	            throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid stop-loss distance");
371	        }
372	
373	        BigDecimal riskModelQuantity = riskAmount.divide(riskPerUnit, MC);
374	
375	        final boolean walletExecuted;
376	        try {
377	            ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
378	                    executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price());
379	            walletExecuted = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price(), freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(), effectivePositionPercent,
380	                    "Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "] " + executionDecision.explanation()
381	                            + " DecisionPrice=" + executionSignal.getLatestPrice()
382	                            + ", executionPrice=" + freshExecutionPrice.price()
383	                            + ", priceObservedAt=" + freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(),
384	                    executionDecision.source(),
385	                    entryQuality.score());
386	        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
387	            log.error("Automatic wallet BUY failed for signal {}: {}", executionSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
388	            return Optional.empty();
389	        }
390	        if (!walletExecuted) {
391	            log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but wallet controls declined execution; no paper position was created.", executionSignal.getId());
392	            return Optional.empty();
393	        }
394	
395	        BigDecimal executedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
396	                .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(executionSignal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
397	                .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
398	                .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
399	                .orElse(riskModelQuantity);
400	
401	        executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, executionDecision);
402	        PaperPosition position = positionRepository.save(PaperPosition.builder()
403	                .symbol(symbol)
404	                .side(PositionSide.BUY)
405	                .status(PositionStatus.OPEN)
406	                .quantity(executedQuantity)
407	                .entryPrice(freshExecutionPrice.price())
408	                .stopLoss(executionSignal.getStopLoss())
409	                .takeProfit(executionSignal.getTakeProfit())
410	                .signal(executionSignal)
411	                .entryReason(executionSignal.getExplanation() + " | Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "]: " + executionDecision.explanation() + " Effective position " + effectivePositionPercent + "%.")
412	                .openedAt(Instant.now())
413	                .build());
414	        return Optional.of(position);
415	    }

417	    /** Kept for compatibility with older callers. */
418	    @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
419	    public Optional<PaperPosition> openFromSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
420	        return processSignal(signal);
421	    }
422	
423	    @Transactional
424	    public PaperPosition close(Long positionId, BigDecimal exitPrice) {
425	        PaperPosition position = positionRepository.findById(positionId)
426	                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Position not found"));
427	
428	        if (position.getStatus() != PositionStatus.OPEN) {
429	            throw new IllegalStateException("Position is already closed");
430	        }
431	
432	        return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
433	                exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
434	                "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
435	    }
436	
437	    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
438	    public List<PaperPosition> list() {
439	        return positionRepository.findTop100ByOrderByOpenedAtDesc();
440	    }
441	
442	    private PaperPosition closeFromSignal(
443	            PaperPosition position,
444	            TradeSignal signal,
445	            PositionStatus status,
446	            String closeReason,
447	            String explanation
448	    ) {
449	        return completeClose(position, signal.getLatestPrice(), status, closeReason, explanation, signal);
450	    }
451	
452	    private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
453	            PaperPosition position,
454	            TradeSignal signal,
455	            DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
456	    ) {
457	        BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
458	        BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
459	                .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
460	        // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
461	        BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
462	                .add(remainingPnl, MC);
463	
464	        position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
465	        position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
466	        position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
467	        position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
468	        position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
469	        position.setExitSignal(signal);
470	        position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
471	        PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
472	
473	        // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
474	        // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
475	        executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
476	
477	        try {
478	            walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
479	        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
480	            log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
481	        }
482	        // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
483	        productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
484	        return saved;
485	    }
486	
487	    private PaperPosition completeClose(
488	            PaperPosition position,
489	            BigDecimal exitPrice,
490	            PositionStatus status,
491	            String closeReason,
492	            String explanation,
493	            TradeSignal exitSignal
494	    ) {
495	        BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
496	                .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
497	        // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
498	        BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
499	                .add(remainingPnl, MC);
500	
501	        position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
502	        position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
503	        position.setStatus(status);
504	        position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
505	        position.setExitReason(explanation);
506	        position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
507	        position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
508	        PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
509	
510	        // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
511	        // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
512	        executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
513	
514	        if (exitSignal != null) {
515	            try {
516	                // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
517	                // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
518	                // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
519	                // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
520	                if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
521	                    walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
522	                } else {
523	                    walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
524	                }
525	            } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
526	                log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
527	            }
528	        }
529	
530	        // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
531	        // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
532	        productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
533	        return saved;
534	    }
535	
536	    private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
537	        if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
538	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
539	        }
540	        return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
541	    }
542	
543	    private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {
544	        Instant startOfDay = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay().toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
545	        BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);
546	        BigDecimal maximumLoss = properties.paperAccountBalance()
547	                .multiply(properties.maxDailyLossPercent(), MC)
548	                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
549	                .negate();
550	
551	        if (pnl.compareTo(maximumLoss) <= 0) {
552	            throw new IllegalStateException("Daily loss limit reached");
553	        }
554	    }
555	}

16	/** FIX-122: evaluation-local history selection. Spring singleton state is thread-bound
17	 * and restored in finally; open-position additions never enter this scope. Replay uses
18	 * its own executed stops, never Production history. No wallet lock or balance writes.
19	 */
20	@Service
21	@Slf4j
22	public class StopLossEvidenceService {
23	    private final StopLossEvidenceStore store;
24	    private final ExecutionReplayScope replay;
25	    private final ObjectMapper mapper;
26	    private final boolean enabled;
27	    private final Clock clock;
28	    private final ThreadLocal<Context> active = new ThreadLocal<>();
29	
30	    @Autowired
31	    public StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
32	                                  ObjectMapper mapper, @Value("${trading.fix122.enabled:true}") boolean enabled) {
33	        this(store,replay,mapper,enabled,Clock.systemUTC());
34	    }
35	
36	    StopLossEvidenceService(StopLossEvidenceStore store, ExecutionReplayScope replay,
37	                           ObjectMapper mapper, boolean enabled, Clock clock) {
38	        this.store=store; this.replay=replay; this.mapper=mapper; this.enabled=enabled; this.clock=clock;
39	    }
40	
41	    private boolean enabled() { return replay.active() ? replay.fix122Enabled() : enabled; }
42	    private Instant now() { return replay.active() ? replay.referenceInstant() : clock.instant(); }
43	    private StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary resolve(String symbol, Instant at) {
44	        return replay.active() ? replay.stopBoundary(symbol, at) : store.latest(symbol, at);
45	    }
46	    public boolean hasBoundary() { return active.get()!=null && active.get().boundary!=null; }
47	
48	    public ExecutionDecision evaluate(TradeSignal signal, int allocation, Supplier<ExecutionDecision> action) {
49	        if (allocation>0 || signal==null || signal.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
50	        return scoped(signal, () -> new SetupWakeupEvaluation(signal, action.get())).decision();
51	    }
52	
53	    public SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup(TradeSignal trigger, int allocation, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
54	        if (allocation>0 || trigger==null || trigger.getGeneratedAt()==null || !enabled()) return action.get();
55	        return scoped(trigger, action);
56	    }
57	
58	    private SetupWakeupEvaluation scoped(TradeSignal trigger, Supplier<SetupWakeupEvaluation> action) {
59	        Context previous=active.get();
60	        Context c=new Context(trigger, now(), replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
61	        active.set(c);
62	        try {
63	            try { c.boundary=resolve(trigger.getSymbol(), c.at); }
64	            catch (RuntimeException error) {
65	                log.warn("[FIX-122] BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE symbol={} signalId={}", trigger.getSymbol(), trigger.getId(), error);
66	                c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
67	                ExecutionDecision d=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
68	                        "Stop history could not be checked. Entry deferred to the next normal evaluation.");
69	                audit(c,d,"EVALUATION");
70	                return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger,d);
71	            }
72	            SetupWakeupEvaluation result;
73	            if ("1m".equals(trigger.getInterval()) && !eligible(trigger,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) {
74	                result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(trigger, ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
75	                        "Current entry candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
76	            } else {
77	                try { result=action.get(); }
78	                catch (StaleCurrent ignored) {
79	                    result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(c.current,ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_CURRENT_CANDLE_STALE",
80	                            "Wake-up timing candle is not eligible after the completed stop-loss."));
81	                }
82	            }
83	            if (result!=null && result.decision()!=null) {
84	                result=new SetupWakeupEvaluation(result.executionSignal(), result.decision()
85	                        .withStopBoundary(new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Stamp(c.boundary)));
86	            }
87	            audit(c,result==null?null:result.decision(),"EVALUATION");
88	            return result==null?SetupWakeupEvaluation.none():result;
89	        } finally {
90	            if(previous==null) active.remove(); else active.set(previous);
91	        }
92	    }
93	
94	    public void requireCurrent(TradeSignal signal) {
95	        if(active.get()!=null) active.get().current=signal;
96	        if(!eligible(signal,"CURRENT_SIGNAL")) throw new StaleCurrent();
97	    }
98	
99	    public boolean eligible(TradeSignal signal, String purpose) {
100	        Context c=active.get();
101	        if(c==null || c.boundary==null) return true;
102	        String reason=signal==null?"LINEAGE_UNRESOLVED":StopLossEvidencePolicy.exclusion(
103	                signal.getInterval(),signal.getCandleOpenTime(),signal.getGeneratedAt(),c.at,c.boundary);
104	        if(reason==null) return true;
105	        Map<String,Object> item=new LinkedHashMap<>();
106	        item.put("purpose",purpose); item.put("reason",reason);
107	        if(signal!=null) {
108	            item.put("signalId",signal.getId()); item.put("interval",signal.getInterval());
109	            item.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(signal.getCandleOpenTime()));
110	            item.put("decision",String.valueOf(signal.getDecision()));
111	        }
112	        if(!c.excluded.contains(item)) c.excluded.add(item);
113	        return false;
114	    }
115	
116	    /** Read again at execution-price approval. A newly committed stop invalidates the
117	     * old approval. This is not a repair of the separate wallet transaction race.
118	     */
119	    public ExecutionDecision recheck(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approved) {
120	        if(approved==null || !approved.allowed() || approved.stopBoundary()==null || !enabled()) return approved;
121	        Context c=new Context(signal,now(),replay.active()?replay.runId():null);
122	        ExecutionDecision result=approved;
123	        try {
124	            c.boundary=resolve(signal.getSymbol(),c.at);
125	            if(!Objects.equals(approved.stopBoundary().boundary(),c.boundary)) {
126	                c.status="BOUNDARY_CHANGED";
127	                result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_CHANGED",
128	                        "A stop-loss boundary changed after qualification. Wait for a fresh evaluation.", approved.evidence());
129	            }
130	        } catch(RuntimeException error) {
131	            c.status="BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE";
132	            result=ExecutionDecision.reject("FIX122_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE",
133	                    "Stop history unavailable at price revalidation. Entry deferred.",approved.evidence());
134	            log.warn("[FIX-122] RECHECK_UNAVAILABLE symbol={}",signal.getSymbol(),error);
135	        }
136	        audit(c,result,"PRICE_RECHECK");
137	        return result;
138	    }
139	
140	    private void audit(Context c,ExecutionDecision d,String stage) {
141	        Map<String,Object> p=new LinkedHashMap<>();
142	        p.put("revision","FIX-122"); p.put("source",c.runId==null?"PRODUCTION":"REPLAY");
143	        p.put("status",c.status!=null?c.status:c.boundary==null?"NOT_APPLICABLE":
144	                c.excluded.isEmpty()?"APPLIED_NO_EXCLUSIONS":"APPLIED_EXCLUSIONS");
145	        p.put("signalId",c.current.getId()); p.put("candleOpenTime",String.valueOf(c.current.getCandleOpenTime()));
146	        p.put("triggerSignalId",c.trigger.getId());
147	        p.put("stopExecutionId",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executionId());
148	        p.put("stopAt",c.boundary==null?null:c.boundary.executedAt().toString());
149	        p.put("excluded",c.excluded);
150	        p.put("allowed",d!=null&&d.allowed()); p.put("route",d==null?null:d.source());
151	        p.put("code",d==null?"NO_WAKEUP":d.code()); p.put("explanation",d==null?null:d.explanation());
152	        if(d!=null && d.evidence()!=null) {
153	            p.put("buyCount",d.evidence().buyCount()); p.put("watchCount",d.evidence().watchCount());
154	            p.put("evidenceScore",d.evidence().evidenceScore()); p.put("health",d.evidence().opportunityHealth());
155	            p.put("positionPercent",d.positionPercent());
156	        }
157	        try { store.append(c.runId,c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.at,stage,mapper.writeValueAsString(p)); }
158	        catch(Exception error) { log.error("[FIX-122] DIAGNOSTIC_PERSIST_FAILED symbol={} signalId={}",
159	                c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),error); }
160	        if(c.boundary!=null || c.status!=null) log.info(
161	                "[FIX-122] {} symbol={} signalId={} stop={} excluded={} code={} allowed={}",stage,
162	                c.trigger.getSymbol(),c.current.getId(),c.boundary,c.excluded.size(),p.get("code"),p.get("allowed"));
163	    }
164	
165	    private static final class StaleCurrent extends RuntimeException {}
166	    private static final class Context {
167	        final TradeSignal trigger; TradeSignal current; final Instant at; final Long runId;
168	        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary boundary; String status;
169	        final List<Map<String,Object>> excluded=new ArrayList<>();
170	        Context(TradeSignal trigger,Instant at,Long runId) { this.trigger=trigger;this.current=trigger;this.at=at;this.runId=runId; }
171	    }
172	}

---src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/StopLossEvidenceStore.java:1-100---
1	package com.crypto.execution.service;
2	
3	import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
4	import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
5	import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
6	import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
7	import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
8	import javax.sql.DataSource;
9	import java.sql.Timestamp;
10	import java.time.Instant;
11	import java.util.List;
12	import java.util.Map;
13	
14	/** FIX-122: bounded, non-locking committed ledger reads; no managed-position row locks.
15	 * STOP_LOSS is the terminal reason written by executeSignalLinkedExit. The distinct
16	 * POSITION_STOP_LOSS and partial-harvest reasons are deliberately not included.
17	 */
18	@Service
19	public class StopLossEvidenceStore {
20	    private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
21	    private final TransactionTemplate read;
22	    private final TransactionTemplate write;
23	
24	    public StopLossEvidenceStore(DataSource dataSource, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
25	        jdbc = new JdbcTemplate(dataSource);
26	        jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
27	        read = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
28	        read.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
29	        read.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
30	        read.setReadOnly(true);
31	        read.setTimeout(2);
32	        write = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
33	        write.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
34	        write.setTimeout(2);
35	    }
36	
37	    public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary latest(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
38	        return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
39	                SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
40	                WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
41	                  AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
42	                ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
43	                """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
44	                rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))
45	                .stream().findFirst().orElse(null));
46	    }
47	
48	    public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
49	        // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
50	        // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
51	        write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
52	                INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
53	                VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
54	                """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
55	    }
56	
57	    public List<Map<String,Object>> replayDetails(long runId) {
58	        return jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id=? ORDER BY id", runId);
59	    }
60	
61	    public List<Map<String,Object>> productionDetails(String symbol, Instant from, Instant to) {
62	        return jdbc.queryForList("""
63	                SELECT * FROM fix122_evaluation WHERE test_run_id IS NULL AND symbol=?
64	                AND evaluated_at>=? AND evaluated_at<=? ORDER BY id
65	                """, symbol, Timestamp.from(from), Timestamp.from(to));
66	    }
67	}

---src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.java:1-190---
1	package com.crypto.wallet.service;
2	
3	import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
4	
5	import java.math.BigDecimal;
6	import java.math.RoundingMode;
7	
8	/**
9	 * Pure wallet BUY sizing/eligibility policy shared by production and Proven/Regression.
10	 * Persistence is intentionally outside this class; all reserve/budget/allocation math lives here.
11	 */
12	@Component
13	public class WalletExecutionSizingPolicy {
14	    private static final BigDecimal ZERO = BigDecimal.ZERO;
15	    private static final int SCALE = 12;
16	
17	    public Plan plan(BigDecimal availableUsdt, BigDecimal minimumReserve, BigDecimal dailyTradeBudget,
18	                     int requestedPositionPercent, int currentAllocatedPercent,
19	                     boolean newPosition, int maximumNewPositions, int executedNewPositions,
20	                     BigDecimal price) {
21	        int requested = Math.max(1, Math.min(100, requestedPositionPercent));
22	        int current = Math.max(0, currentAllocatedPercent);
23	        int normalized = Math.min(requested, Math.max(0, 100 - current));
24	        if (normalized <= 0) return Plan.reject("ALLOCATION_FULL");
25	        if (newPosition && maximumNewPositions > 0 && executedNewPositions >= maximumNewPositions) {
26	            return Plan.reject("DAILY_POSITION_LIMIT");
27	        }
28	        BigDecimal available = nvl(availableUsdt).subtract(nvl(minimumReserve)).max(ZERO);
29	        BigDecimal spend = nvl(dailyTradeBudget).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(normalized))
30	                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), SCALE, RoundingMode.DOWN);
31	        if (spend.signum() <= 0) return Plan.reject("ZERO_BUDGET");
32	        if (available.compareTo(spend) < 0) return Plan.reject("INSUFFICIENT_USDT_ABOVE_RESERVE");
33	        if (price == null || price.signum() <= 0) return Plan.reject("INVALID_PRICE");
34	        BigDecimal quantity = spend.divide(price, SCALE, RoundingMode.DOWN);
35	        if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return Plan.reject("ZERO_QUANTITY");
36	        return new Plan(true, normalized, spend, quantity, "APPROVED");
37	    }
38	
39	    public BigDecimal initialDailyBudget(BigDecimal availableUsdt, BigDecimal minimumReserve,
40	                                         BigDecimal baseTradeAmount, int maximumNewPositions) {
41	        BigDecimal tradable = nvl(availableUsdt).subtract(nvl(minimumReserve)).max(ZERO);
42	        if (tradable.signum() <= 0) return ZERO;
43	        return maximumNewPositions == 0
44	                ? nvl(baseTradeAmount).min(tradable)
45	                : tradable.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(maximumNewPositions), SCALE, RoundingMode.DOWN);
46	    }
47	
48	    private BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value) { return value == null ? ZERO : value; }
49	
50	    public record Plan(boolean allowed, int normalizedPositionPercent, BigDecimal spend,
51	                       BigDecimal quantity, String code) {
52	        static Plan reject(String code) { return new Plan(false, 0, ZERO, ZERO, code); }
53	    }
54	}

---src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:1-170---
1	package com.crypto.execution.service;
2	
3	import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
4	import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
5	import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
6	
7	import java.math.BigDecimal;
8	import java.time.Duration;
9	import java.time.Instant;
10	import java.util.Optional;
11	import java.util.Objects;
12	
13	/**
14	 * FIX-056 fresh execution-price authority.
15	 *
16	 * <p>TradeSignal.latestPrice is immutable decision-time evidence and must never be
17	 * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
18	 * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
19	 * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
20	 * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
21	 */
22	@Service
23	@RequiredArgsConstructor
24	public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
25	    private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);
26	
27	    private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
28	    private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
29	
30	    public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
31	        // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
32	        // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
33	        // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
34	        Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
35	                "Execution price reference time is required");
36	
37	        if (replayScope.active()) {
38	            return replayScope.latestPrice()
39	                    .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
40	                    .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
41	                    .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
42	                    .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
43	                    .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
44	        }
45	
46	        return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
47	                .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
48	                .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
49	    }
50	
51	    public record ExecutionPrice(BigDecimal price, Instant observedAt, String source) {}
52	}

---src/main/java/com/crypto/service/RegimeStateService.java:1-150---
1	package com.crypto.service;
2	
3	import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
4	import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
5	import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
6	import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
7	import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
8	
9	import java.time.Instant;
10	import java.util.HashMap;
11	import java.util.List;
12	import java.util.Map;
13	
14	/**
15	 * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
16	 * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
17	 * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
18	 */
19	@Service
20	public class RegimeStateService {
21	    private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
22	    private final int requiredCandles;
23	    // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
24	    // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
25	    private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
26	
27	    public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
28	                              @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
29	        this.replayScope = replayScope;
30	        this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
31	    }
32	
33	    public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
34	                                       MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
35	        if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
36	            MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
37	            return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
38	        }
39	
40	        long runId = replayScope.runId();
41	        String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
42	        RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
43	        MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
44	        if (state == null) {
45	            state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
46	            replayState.put(key, state);
47	            return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
48	        }
49	
50	        MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
51	        MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
52	        int count = state.candidateCount;
53	        boolean promoted = false;
54	
55	        if (detected == confirmed) {
56	            candidate = null;
57	            count = 0;
58	        } else if (detected == candidate) {
59	            count++;
60	        } else {
61	            candidate = detected;
62	            count = 1;
63	        }
64	
65	        if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
66	            confirmed = candidate;
67	            candidate = null;
68	            count = 0;
69	            promoted = true;
70	        }
71	        replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
72	
73	        MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
74	                ? detectedAssessment
75	                : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
76	                    append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
77	                            + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
78	                            + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
79	        return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
80	    }
81	
82	    private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
83	        java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
84	        result.add(value);
85	        return List.copyOf(result);
86	    }
87	
88	    private record RegimeState(MarketRegime confirmed, MarketRegime candidate, int candidateCount, Instant lastCandleTime) {}
89	
90	    public record Decision(MarketRegimeAssessment effectiveAssessment,
91	                           MarketRegime detectedRegime,
92	                           MarketRegime candidateRegime,
93	                           MarketRegime confirmedRegime,
94	                           int candidateCount,
95	                           boolean promoted) {}
96	}

1	# Frozen PDF Artifact Rules
2	
3	Markdown remains the machine-readable source. A PDF is the frozen human-review artifact for major approval gates.
4	
5	Mandatory frozen artifacts when the corresponding stage exists:
6	- `FINAL_TECHNICAL_REVIEW-Rxx.pdf`
7	- `FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION-Rxx.pdf`
8	- `IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT-Rxx.pdf`
9	- `VALIDATION_REPORT-Rxx.pdf`
10	
11	Each frozen PDF must include the source revision, SHA-256 of the exact source Markdown, Codex verdict, Claude verdict, implementation authorization state, and a statement that any material source change invalidates the frozen revision.
12	
13	Use `scripts/freeze-agent-artifact.ps1`. A dual-approved specification is not READY FOR USER REVIEW until its PDF freeze succeeds. PDF generation never grants implementation authorization.

62	    public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
63	        ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
64	        if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
65	
66	        WalletSettings settings = settings();
67	        if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
68	            TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
69	            // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
70	            // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
71	            // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
72	            if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
73	                    && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
74	                    && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
75	                return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
76	                        "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
77	                                + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
78	            }
79	        }
80	
81	        TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
82	        if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
83	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
84	                    "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
85	        }
86	
87	        TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
88	        if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
89	            return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
90	                    "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
91	        }
92	
93	        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
94	        if(!assessment.allowed()) {
95	            recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
96	            audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
97	            return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
98	        }
99	
100	        ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
101	        ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
102	            case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
103	            // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
104	            // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
105	            // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
106	            case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
107	                    executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
108	            case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
109	        };
110	        ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
111	        audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
112	        return result;
113	    }

115	    /**
116	     * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
117	     * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
118	     * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
119	     * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
120	     * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
121	     * normal BUY path would accept.
122	     */
123	    public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
124	        return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
125	    }

38	    public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
39	                       HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
40	                       String finalResult, String reason) {
41	        // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
42	        // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
43	        if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
44	                || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
45	        AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
46	        try {
47	            executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
48	        } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
49	            metrics.auditWriteFailed();
50	            log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
51	                    path, reference.getId());
52	        }
53	    }

64	                      confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
65	                      legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
66	                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
67	                    """,
68	                    p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
69	                    p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
70	                    p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
71	                    HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
72	                    p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6 is not complete or safe for technical-design approval. Its “complete” registry, storage boundary, and staging contract conflict with current Replay code and schema.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The artifact names a non-current run table (`regression_test_run`), while current Replay uses `analysis_test_run` and writes many omitted Replay-owned tables.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Isolation is not fully designed: current paths read `wallet_settings`, query Production `wallet_trade` for a Replay chart, and call the Binance market-data client for minimum-order constraints.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** `ANALYSIS_PARITY` has no defined comparison contract or wired oracle. `ReplayParityValidator` exists but has no caller.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The spec omits active shared BUY behavior: `TradeExecutionValidationService` invokes FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment. The artifact neither registers its dependent classes/audit behavior nor defines Replay treatment.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6 declares `regression_test_run` as Replay-owned output, but the source schema defines `analysis_test_run`; no `regression_test_run` definition was found. ## OBJECTIONS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The artifact fails the repository’s required change-contract granularity: it lacks method-by-method current→new behavior, exact schema objects, transaction/publication/crash-recovery rules, and a complete file registry. [AGENTS.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:339)

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** A revision must provide a verified read/write/dependency matrix covering every Replay output, all account/config/network paths, and all observer/audit paths.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** It must define staging ownership, foreign keys, terminal states, idempotency, publication atomicity, cancellation races, and crash recovery. “Discard staged artifacts” is insufficient while independent transactions exist.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** It must define `ANALYSIS_PARITY`: exact candidate set, match identity, compared fields, ordering/clock evidence, pass/fail criteria, and refusal conditions. Otherwise the label can overstate parity.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** It must explicitly model Production admission outcomes such as data-quality blocks, unavailable history, and historical-only processing rather than treating all non-trades as infrastructure errors or allowing simulated trades for candles Production would not execute.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** It must resolve the scope contradiction: RPR says no Production strategy change, yet registers modifications to shared Production services and the protection path. A safe revision needs explicit invariants, transaction/lock-order regression tests, and measurable Production-freshness acceptance criteria.

## UNKNOWNS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** No database, production runtime, SSH target, or secrets were accessed.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Historical windows may lack the required configuration, ordering, order-book, derivatives, or sentiment provenance. The design must fail closed until the qualifier can prove availability per dependency.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The reviewed artifact SHA-256 is `5C057C181459727E7D62F580F525FDB4392E15BBEAD7A27F5BAA2F3AAADF5A03`; the worktree is materially dirty, so its stated Git baseline alone cannot identify an implementable snapshot.
